Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any nano module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 366. The projective resolution has length at most 123 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on nano structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 366. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 123. QED.

Theorem 219.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)

Statement: Given a nano algebra A over field F with homomorphism theorem conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 491. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 790/2.
